| Weedle          | 9   | 112 |
| **Money:** $108 |

> High-level Weedle can hurt/poison — use Gust/Ember if you have it.

Press **A** on his tile for **hidden Potion**, then north out of forest → Route 2 → Pewter City.

## II9. Pewter City: The Stone Grey City

Home to your first Gym. Check shops first.

| Pewter Poké Mart | Price | Notes                                      |
| ---------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------ |
| Poké Ball        | $200  | Stock if low                               |
| Potion           | $300  |                                            |
| Antidote         | $100  |                                            |
| Paralyze Heal    | $200  | Not needed yet                             |
| Burn Heal        | $250  | Not needed yet                             |
| Awakening        | $200  |                                            |
| Escape Rope      | $550  | Escape dungeons instantly                  |
| Repel            | $100  | Keeps < your lead level away for 100 steps |

Near the Mart, a guy offers Museum tour for $50 — nothing to see, skip.

**Hidden:** Top-left grass patch, stand by fence → search ground for **hidden Poké Ball**.

Exit east → nerd blocks you until you have Brock's badge, escorts you slowly to Gym. Time to beat Brock.

## II10. Brock's Gym

Tiny gym — one trainer before leader.

#### Camper Liam

| Pokémon         | Lv  | Exp |
| --------------- | --- | --- |
| Geodude         | 10  | 183 |
| Sandshrew       | 11  | 219 |
| **Money:** $220 |

> If you beat Liam easily with Water Gun/Vine Whip → ready for Brock.

**Your Pokémon (example)**
| Pokémon | Level |
|---|---|
| Squirtle | 14 |
| Pidgey | 14 |

#### Gym Leader Brock

| Pokémon          | Lv  | Exp |
| ---------------- | --- | --- |
| Geodude          | 12  | 220 |
| Onix             | 14  | 324 |
| **Money:** $1400 |

> **Easy if Bulbasaur/Squirtle:** Vine Whip/Water Gun OHKOs at Lv 12–13. **Charmander:** needs **Metal Claw** (Lv 9) for super-effective. Pidgey/Butterfree weak — Rock Tomb destroys them and lowers Speed. After win: **Boulder Badge** (boosts Attack, lets you use Flash outside battle) + **TM 39 Rock Tomb**.

## II11. The TM Case

Brock also gives the **TM Case** (Key Items). Holds TMs/HMs — now look like CDs placed on Pokémon to teach moves. **TMs = one-use, HMs = infinite**. Not every Pokémon can learn every move.

## III5. The Cerulean "Point of No Return"

Pick your fossil, collect **Antidote** on way out via final ladder to sunlight. Right → two blackbelts (Move Tutors, each teaches **once**): left = **Mega Punch** (contact), right = **Mega Kick** (powerful, inaccurate) — save for later. Further right → **TM 05 Roar**.

Drop off ledge below TM → hidden **Razz Berry** (common in R/S). This ledge is the "Point of No Return" — once you drop, stuck in eastern Kanto until later. Jump it.

# IV. Partyless Boats, Slaves, and the Third Badge

**Mission Objectives**

- Recover TM 28 Dig
- Claim a "free" Bike in Cerulean
- Board the S.S Anne and battle trainers there
- Fight Gary (again)
- Claim HM 01 Cut
- Fight for your Third Badge

After Misty, heal at Center, loop behind it to house with police officer — robbery!

House ransacked: couple's **Dig TM** stolen. Leave via back-wall hole → backyard → "innocent bystander" in Rocket uniform.

#### Team Rocket Grunt

| Pokémon         | Lv  | Exp |
| --------------- | --- | --- |

East bush blocks; go south hedges → ledges with grass patches → bottom: **Pokémon Day-Care**: leave 1 Pokémon (gains 1 Exp/step, learns moves uncontrollably). Cost to retrieve: $100 + $100 × levels gained. Great for Magikarp via Running Shoes.

## IV1. Underground Path

Continue along Route 5 into the gatehouse leading to Saffron City — guard complains he's thirsty and blocks entry (logic!). Next door is a small house with the **Underground Path**. Inside, a girl will trade **Nidoran♂ ↔ Nidoran♀** (FireRed: she gives ♂ for ♀; LeafGreen: opposite). Trade if you want, then head downstairs.

The path is long but simple — just green floor. It's the only way to bypass Saffron for now. At the east end, exit and head far left on Route 6: face right for **Rare Candy** and north for **Sitrus Berry** (your 3rd Rare Candy — save it).

## IV2. Vermilion City — The Port of Exquisite Sunsets

Heal at Center — inside, woman by counter gives **VS. Seeker**. When charged, use it on-screen trainers: **!!** = wants rematch, **X** = no, **!** = not yet fought. Cannot use in caves/buildings; recharges after **100 steps**.

Left of Center → small house → **Fishing Guru**: say you love fishing → **Old Rod** (fish adjacent water for low-level Magikarp).

South/east → **Pokémon Fan Club** — seniors with dolls. Chairman at top babbles about Rapidash then gives **Bike Voucher** (free bike at Cerulean Bike Shop). Backtrack to Cerulean quickly, grab **Bicycle** (register to Select), pick up Spearow near Cerulean if needed (one for party, one for future trade).

**Hidden:** Bottom-left corner of Vermilion beach sand → **hidden Max Elixir**.

| Paralyze Heal       | $200  |                         |
| Awakening           | $250  |                         |
| Ice Heal            | $250  | Not needed yet (no Ice) |
| Repel               | $350  |                         |

Last task: dock south of Mart — guard checks ticket then boards **S.S Anne**.

## IV5. The Mew Delivery Truck

East of S.S Anne dock, surfable land holds a **truck** (Mew truck from R/B). No Mew here (11-year rumor false), but cool Easter egg. Can't access after ship leaves unless you trade in a Pokémon that already knows **Cut** to skip next section, then Surf back later.

## IV6. The Useless HM 01 — Cut

Continue up hall → stairs → captain's room: check trash can (seasick mess), talk to captain → massage → cures seasickness → **HM 01 Cut** (terrible in battle, but needed to cut small bushes and access Lt. Surge's Gym). Teach to HM slave, not main team.

## IV7. A Farfetch'd Solution

Leave ship — it sails away forever. Heal at Vermilion Center. Take your old **Farfetch'd** (from earlier trade) and teach it Cut (via TM Case). Also grab **Abra** caught earlier for next trade.

## IV8. Diglett's Cave

Can skip to Gym, but for advantage/Exp follow this:

East of Vermilion → cave entrance → ladder. Cave is straight line (bike works). Encounters: **Diglett** common, **Dugtrio** rare ~Lv 30 (evolved). Dugtrio has **Arena Trap** (no flee unless Flying/Levitate). Catching Diglett recommended (author skips for challenge). Exit back to Kanto.

## IV9. A Once-Seen Trade?

Exit lands on **Route 2** (Viridian Forest area) via Diglett's Cave. South → previously inaccessible house → man trades **Mr. Mime for Abra** (great: Mr. Mime has **Calm Mind** + needed for Dex; Abra line better evolved but early Abra useless).

Continue south into gatehouse → Oak's aide: with **≥10 Pokédex entries** gives **HM 05 Flash**.

## IV10. A "Flash"-back to Ugly Times

We've had **HM 05 Flash** for 10+ years across 11 games (13 with D/P). Only used **once** outside battle — buy a flashlight! In battle: **Flash — Power —, Acc 70, PP 20** — "A blast of light is sent out to blind foes. Also illuminates caves." — lowers foe Accuracy. HM can't be deleted for ages; accuracy down moves rarely viable competitively.

> Three dots say it all. ... How does a huge light miss? Close eyes?

## IV11. A Short Pewter City Visit

Leave gatehouse south → find **Ether**; further south → **Paralyze Heal**. Back to Diglett's Cave, cut small tree → re-enter Pewter City.

North to **Pokémon Museum**: cut tree near entrance → back door. Inside: man teaches **Seismic Toss** (damage = user level); bookshelf → **Brock's Fame Checker** final entry; top corner man gives **Old Amber** (revive at Cinnabar) — must take it.

Optional: In Viridian City behind cut bush → man teaches **Dream Eater** (Leech Life vs. sleeping). Remember for later. Return via Diglett's Cave to Vermilion.

# V. There's Evil Afoot! And the Fourth Badge!

**Mission Objectives**

- Create an HM Slave
- Battle to and through Rock Tunnel
- Defeat your rival again
- Enter Rocket Game Corner
- Find and defeat Giovanni
- Battle for your fourth badge

Talk to everyone in Gym for Fame Checker (5 entries, last on Pokémon Center bookshelf). Get **Meowth/Oddish** (or Abra) as HM slave for **Flash + Cut**. Stock **Escape Ropes + Repels + Super Potions** at Mart.

Hug right fence in Cerulean without entering → pathway inside city → small **Cut tree**.

## V3. Lavender Town — The Noble Purple Town

Small purple hillside town.

| Lavender Poké Mart | Price | Notes                                  |
| ------------------ | ----- | -------------------------------------- |
| Great Ball         | $600  | Better catch rate — replace Poké Balls |

| Revive             | $1500 | New                                    |

Visible from Route 10: **Pokémon Tower** (honors dead Pokémon). South of Tower: **Volunteer Center** (Mr. Fuji head, absent) — talk to little girl for Fame Checker; table → coupon. Further south: **Name Rater** (rename only your caught Pokémon, not traded).

Briefly enter **Pokémon Tower** → 2F save → north → Gary battle!

## V4. Gary Battle #5

**Your Pokémon (example)**
| Pokémon | Level |
|---|---|
| Wartortle | 35 |
| Pidgeotto | 33 |
| Dugtrio | 29 |

**Gary's Pokémon**
| Your Starter | Team |
|---|---|
| Bulbasaur | Charmeleon Lv25 + Pidgeotto Lv25 (604) + Gyarados Lv22 + Kadabra Lv20 (621) + Exeggcute Lv23 |
| Charmander | Wartortle Lv25 + Pidgeotto Lv25 + Exeggcute Lv22 + Kadabra Lv20 + Growlithe Lv23 (429) |
| Squirtle | Ivysaur Lv25 (682) + Pidgeotto Lv25 + Growlithe Lv22 + Kadabra Lv20 + Gyarados Lv23 (1054) |
| **Money:** $900 |

> No more hacked Raticate — teams now diverge by starter. **Exeggcute:** Stun Spore → burn with Flying/Fire. **Gyarados:** high stats, Water + Thrash (confuses after several turns) — Electric needed. **Growlithe:** minor, Water to kill.

After, Gary claims he caught Cubone and couldn't find Marowak mother (no Marowak in Tower). Ghost now blocks top of Tower — wild "Ghost" encounters: your Pokémon too scared to attack, Balls miss, must flee. Leave Tower. South route (Route 11) still blocked by **Snorlax**. Only way is west.

# VI. The Long Road to Fuchsia City, Wherein Lies the Fifth Badge

**Mission Objectives**

- Obtain HM 02 Fly
- Finish Pokemon Tower
- Choose one of two routes to reach Fuchsia City
- Enter the Safari Zone
- Obtain more HMs
- Battle for your Fifth Badge

Now with Silph Scope we can clear Tower. First grab Fly: west out of Celedon → **Snorlax** blocks → nearby **Cut tree** → battle:

#### Young Couple Lea & Jed (Double Battle)

| Pokémon          | Lv  | Exp  |
| ---------------- | --- | ---- |
| Rapidash         | 29  | 1190 |
| Ninetales        | 29  | 1104 |
| **Money:** $1624 |

> Water/Ground KO both.

Enter left guardhouse → man surprised you found secret area → out left door → house with reclusive girl → **HM 02 Fly** (for secrecy).

Teach Fly to HM slave or main Flying; use to fly to visited towns — **Fly to Lavender**.

## VI2. Two Roads to Fuchsia City

You now have **Poké Flute** — can wake both Snorlax. Two block routes to Fuchsia, your choice:

- **Southern routes (Routes 12–15, Silence Bridge):** Littered with unavoidable trainers, very long path to Fuchsia, but you're near it from Lavender.
- **Cycling Road (west of Celedon):** Downhill bike-only road — only Bikers, **every trainer skippable** but good Exp, lots of them.

Author chooses **Cycling Road first** (fastest). You can do Silence Bridge now or later.

## VI3. The Downhill Ride of Cycling Road

Grass patches with fresh encounters. Fly to Celedon if taking this route.

West of city → Snorlax blocks. Use Poké Flute next to it → wakes and attacks! **Lv 30** Snorlax, ~150 HP, high Def/Sp.Def, high Atk, knows **Body Slam** (may paralyze) + **Rest** (full heal). Capture recommended (if you KO, other Snorlax remains; if you catch, you can KO the other). After battle, Itemfinder on spot → **Leftovers** (1/16 HP/turn when held). Gatehouse next door: upstairs Oak's aide with **≥40 Pokédex entries** → **Amulet Coin** (double money when holder participates).

Guard requires bike — enter **Cycling Road** (6 top bikers → then more south).

## VI5. Fuchsia City — Behold! It's Passion Pink!

Center blocked by ledge — approach from left (Cycling Road side) via stairs. Heal, then houses near Center:

- **Move Deleter** (next house) — delete any move forever (until HM/TM relearn) — remove Flash from slave now.
- Next house: **Bill's grandpa** → Bill Fame Checker section.
- Two houses right: left = **Safari Zone Warden** (missing false teeth, unintelligible), right = **Fishing Guru** with **Good Rod** (obsolete if you have Super Rod — fish Gyarados in backyard pond).
- North winding path: caged Pokémon exhibit (Pokedex pages) — Cut shortcut.

Follow light-green path east → north building: Safari Zone entrance.

| Fuchsia Poké Mart | Price |
| ----------------- | ----- |
| Ultra Ball        | $1200 |
| Great Ball        | $600  |
| Super Potion      | $700  |
| Revive            | $1500 |
| Full Heal         | $600  |
| Max Repel         | $700  |

> **Ultra Balls** much higher catch rate than Great — buy them; discard old Potions/Poké Balls/Repels.

Follow light-green path east → north → Safari Zone (need 2 HMs).

## VI6. All You Can Capture! The Safari Zone!

Must enter to obtain 2 HMs (but fun). Pay **$500** → **30 Safari Balls**. You have **600 steps** — no more; if Balls run out, game ends on spot. Many exclusives only here.

Safari mechanics: no Pokémon sent out — 4 options: **Rock** (angry, flee ↑ capture ↑), **Bait** (flee ↓ capture ↓), **Ball**, **Run**. Start Menu → **Retire** (instant end). Cannot save → no cheap reset.

4 areas: **Center Area** (start), **Area 1, Area 2, Area 3** (contains False Teeth + Secret House).

**Path to Secret House (items en route):** From Center → NE exit → Area 1: up stairs to ledge → **Leaf Stone**; down far-left stairs → **TM 11 Sunny Day** → next room. Area 2: down path → **TM 47 Steel Wing** (strong Steel for Flyers) → Rest House talk old man → Koga Fame Checker entry.

Steps left ~150 to Secret House — if low, exit/re-enter direct. Linear loop: bottom-left of Area 2 → Area 3: sign "Warden has lost his teeth..." → 3 steps south → **False Teeth**; a bit south → **TM 32 Double Team**; west → **Secret House** → man congratulates → **HM 03 Surf** (amazing Water attack; needs Koga badge to use). Retire or continue catching.

Recall Warden's house east of Center → return False Teeth → rewards **HM 04 Strength** (push boulders). Move boulder in his house → **Rare Candy** behind.

Fuchsia Gym next.

# VII. Crossways of Kanto, More Schemes, and the Sixth Badge

**Mission Objectives**

- See the Mew Delivery Truck
- Choose any path and enter Saffron City
- Infiltrate Silph Co.
- Defeat Giovanni once again
- Rip through the Fighting Dojo
- Fight for your Sixth Badge

With Surf (teach to Water-type, not slave — best Water attack aside from Hydro Pump). Don't head directly Saffron yet — Fly to Vermillion first.

## VII1. The Mew Delivery Truck — Revisited

Surf east from S.S Anne dock (music changes) → stretch of land with truck marked **"S"** red. Mystery persists 11 years — nothing interactable; if you find something, email author (likely lie). South of truck: **Lava Cookie** (random).

## VII2. Tea Snatchers

Saffron is Kanto center — 4 gates (Celedon east, Vermillion north, Lavender west, Cerulean south). Pass gate → guard notices **Tea** from Celedon Mansion old lady (1F). If you don't have Tea, return to grab it. He "magically" warps to other guards — now free passage (Fly makes it moot).

## VII3. Saffron City — Shining, Golden Land of Commerce

Team Rocket grunts wander (won't fight in city). SW corner: **Pokémon Center**. Several houses east: **Mr. Psychic** → **TM 29 Psychic** (don't waste on Psychic-types who learn it naturally).

North street center: **Silph Co.** (tech leading) — Grunt snoozing at front. Further north: Rocket blocks several buildings; two Gyms NE corner: right = Sabrina (6th badge), left = Fighting Dojo (rare Pokémon) — ignore for now (Grunt blocks Sabrina). South of Gyms: **Poké Mart**:

| Saffron Poké Mart | Price | Notes |
| ----------------- | ----- | ----- |
| Great Ball        | $600  |       |
| Hyper Potion      | $1200 |       |
| Revive            | $1500 |       |
| Full Heal         | $600  |       |
| Paralyze Heal     | $200  |       |
| Escape Rope       | $550  |       |
| Max Repel         | $700  |       |

> Downgrade from Fuchsia — no Ultra Balls; Hyper Potion poor value vs Celedon Fresh Water ($200–$350 Lemonade route: 3 Lemonades 240 HP for $1150 vs Hyper 200 HP for $1200). Buy Full Heals/Revives for Silph.

Enter Silph Co. if Grunt not blocking (must have beaten Koga).

## VII7. The Master Ball

Talk to President → rewards **Master Ball** (never fails — one only, don't mis-throw!). Northwest of Silph: **Copycat's house** → copies you, laughs — give **Poké Doll** → teaches **Mimic**.

Heal → **Fighting Dojo** (NE Saffron left of Sabrina).

Reward choice: **Hitmonlee left vs Hitmonchan right**:

|              | Hitmonlee                                | Hitmonchan                                       |
| ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| **High**     | Attack (Fighting = Attack-based)         | HP, Speed, slightly higher Defense               |
| **Low**      | Special Attack very low, Speed mediocre  | Attack lower than Lee, Special Attack even lower |
| **Movepool** | Literally crap (few good level/TM moves) | Good (Elemental Punches) but needs Sp.Atk        |

Take one for Dex anyway. Heal → next door → **Sabrina's Gym**.

# VIII. Empty Mansions, Idiot Questions, and the Seventh Badge

**Mission Objectives**

- Find a use for Surf and make way for Cinnabar Island
- Revive the fossils you have collected
- Wander Pokemon Mansion and find Secret Key
- Battle for the seventh badge

## VIII1. Visit Mom

Two choices to Cinnabar (middle of ocean): **Fuchsia** is longer via Seafoam Islands cavern; **Pallet Town** is directly north with few Swimmers — author chooses Pallet (Seafoam covered post-game). Head to Pallet: talk to Mom (heal, doesn't care), talk to Oak for Dex evaluation, then Surf south.

## VIII2. Cinnabar Island — The Fiery Town of Burning Desire

Tiny island: only 5 buildings (Mansion, Lab, Blaine's Gym, Center, Mart). Girl near sign → Blaine Fame Checker entry.

| Cinnabar Poké Mart | Price | Notes             |
| ------------------ | ----- | ----------------- |
| Ultra Ball         | $1200 | New standard      |
| Great Ball         | $600  |                   |
| Hyper Potion       | $1200 |                   |
| Revive             | $1500 |                   |
| Full Heal          | $600  |                   |
| Escape Rope        | $550  |                   |
| Max Repel          | $700  | Very helpful soon |

Sell junk (Potions, old Balls) — stock Ultra/Max Repel.

## VIII3. Reviving Some Ancient Pokemon

Center → heal → **Pokemon Lab** west of Center (3 rooms):

- **Room 1 (right):** Man trades **Electrode for Raichu** (if you don't use Pikachu line, good for Voltorb line); lady trades **Tangela for Venonat** (Tangela common elsewhere; holds Stardust — sell).
- **Room 2:** Tutor teaches **Metronome** (random move: Snorlax can Fly or Self-Destruct or Splash — novelty).
- **Room 3:** Man trades **Seel for Ponyta** (neither seen yet — need Ponyta soon; Seel common Seafoam, dumb trade).

Also: scientist with revival machine → hand **Helix/Dome Fossil** → leave/re-enter → returns **Omanyte (Helix) / Kabuto (Dome) Lv5**; also **Old Amber → Aerodactyl Lv5**.

Gym locked — need **Secret Key** from Mansion next door.

## X2. The Path to Victory Road

Gatehouse guards demand badges sequentially: **Boulder** (Brock) → **Cascade** (Misty) → **Thunder** (Surge). Left loop hidden **Leppa Berry** spot → next guard wants **Rainbow** (Erika) → next **Soul** (Koga) → Surf across water → swimming guard wants **Marsh** (Sabrina). Grass patches:

| Sandslash        | Rare (LG)   |                                                             |
| Ekans            | Common (FR) |                                                             |
| Arbok            | Rare (FR)   |                                                             |
| Primeape         | Common      |                                                             |
| Mankey           | Rare        |                                                             |

Grass north hidden **Aspear Berry**. Next guards: **Volcano** (Blaine) → **Earth** (Giovanni) → entrance to Victory Road. Heal/buy before entering.

# XI. Indigo Plateau and the Elite 4

**Mission Objectives**

- Defeat the Elite 4

Final shopping + Center in reception. You **cannot leave** until you win/lose — no Centers/shops between battles.

| Indigo Plateau Shop | Price |
| ------------------- | ----- |
| Ultra Ball          | $1200 |
| Great Ball          | $600  |
| Max Potion          | $2500 |
| Revive              | $1500 |
| Full Restore        | $3000 |
| Full Heal           | $550  |
| Max Repel           | $700  |

> Hyper vs Max Potion: Hyper $1200 200 HP → Max $2500 full — Hyper better unless >200 HP (author Lapras only). Full Heals essential — you **will** be statused.

North entrance to Elite 4.

**Your final team (example)**
| Pokémon | Level |
|---|---|
| Blastoise | 61 (underleveled, Elite 4 higher) |
| Pidgeot | 61 |
| Dugtrio | 60 |
| Flareon | 61 |
| Lapras | 61 |

## XI1. Elite 4 Lorelei — Ice Master

Reward $5400.

#### Dewgong 52 — Thick Fat, Water/Ice — 1960 Exp

- Attacks: Surf, Hail (5 turns, 1/16 HP chip non-Ice), Ice Beam (10% freeze), Safeguard (no status 5 turns)
  > Leads with Hail → Safeguard/Surf/Ice Beam. Thick Fat irrelevant. Lapras walls both STABs → Thunderbolt destroys team.

#### Cloyster 51 — Shell Armor, Water/Ice — 2218 Exp

- Attacks: Spikes (switch damage), Hail, Dive (Water Dig), Protect
  > Hail → Protect stall. Shell Armor blocks crits. Thunderbolt / huge Defense means only Electric works.

#### Slowbro 52 — Oblivious, Water/Psychic — 1827 Exp

- Attacks: Yawn (sleep next turn), Ice Beam, Surf, Amnesia (+2 Sp.Def)
  > Oblivious blocks Attract. Yawn → Amnesia spam to max SpDef → almost unbeatable → kill fast before boosts; use Poke Flute if slept. Thunderbolt/Dark.

#### Jynx 54 — Oblivious, Ice/Psychic — 1584 Exp

- Attacks: Attract (50% fail male), Ice Punch, Lovely Kiss (Sleep), Doubleslap (weak)
  > Almost always Lovely Kiss first (sleep). Attract rarely used luckily. Both attacks weak (should be Ice Beam). You'll be asleep every other turn — annoying but low damage.

#### Lapras 54 — Water Absorb, Water/Ice — 2533 Exp

- Attacks: Surf, Ice Beam (10% freeze), Confuse Ray, Body Slam (30% paralyze)
  > Huge HP (~220) → 2–3 Thunderbolts. Has Full Restores when red. Opens Confuse Ray → Surf/Ice Beam/Body Slam. Don't switch — keep attacking. Full Heal/Thunderbolt.

Heal after, door opens to Bruno.

## XI2. Elite 4 Bruno — Fighting/Rock

Reward $5600.

#### Onix 51 — Rock Head, Rock/Ground — 1179 Exp

- Attacks: Iron Tail, Roar, Rock Tomb, Earthquake
  > Rock Head blocks recoil. One Surf.

#### Onix 54 — Rock Head, Rock/Ground — 1249 Exp

- Attacks: Iron Tail, Earthquake, Sand Tomb, Double-Edge
  > +3 levels, Double-Edge now but still OHKO Surf (Rock Head irrelevant).

#### Hitmonlee 53 — Limber, Fighting — 1578 Exp

- Attacks: Mega Kick (strong Normal no STAB), Foresight (allow Fighting vs Ghost, never used unless you field Ghost or out of PP), Facade (weak unless burned/poisoned/para → doubles ~100), Brick Break (breaks Reflect/Barrier)
  > Limber blocks paralysis. Bring Flying/Psychic but not Defense-down.

#### Hitmonchan 53 — Keen Eye, Fighting — 1590 Exp

- Attacks: Mach Punch (Fighting Quick Attack), Rock Tomb, Sky Uppercut, Counter
  > Usually Mach Punch first → Sky Uppercut; Rock Tomb vs Flying. Psychic/Flying good.

#### Machamp 56 — Guts, Fighting — 2316 Exp

- Attacks: Cross Chop (strongest Fighting), Bulk Up (+1 Atk/Def), Rock Tomb, Scary Face (slow)
  > Massive Attack; only weakness SpDef crap → one Psychic OHKOs even with Bulk Up. Avoid Flying (Rock Tomb). Full power sweeper.

## XI3. Elite 4 Agatha — Ghost/Poison (like Koga)

Reward $5800.

#### Gengar 54 — Levitate, Ghost/Poison — 2197 Exp

- Attacks: Toxic, Confuse Ray, Shadow Punch, Double Team
  > Leads Toxic (hope miss) → Confuse Ray + Double Team untargetable → Shadow Punch vs Psychic (keep them out). Psychic-type attacks vs Poison; Full Heal if Toxic'd (doubling damage).

#### Haunter 54 — Levitate, Ghost/Poison — 1431 Exp

- Attacks: Hypnosis, Dream Eater (heals when you sleep), Curse (halves HP to curse 1/4 per turn), Mean Look
  > Levitate blocks Ground. Curse usually first → if you hit before Curse, it suicides ~half HP. Mean Look rarely used. Don't sleep.

#### Arbok 56 — Intimidate, Poison — 1764 Exp

- Attacks: Sludge Bomb, Iron Tail, Screech, Bite
  > Intimidate −1 Atk → use Special. Psychic destroys; Steel walls all (low damage). Sludge Bomb strong + high poison.

#### Golbat 54 — Guts, Poison/Flying — 1978 Exp

- Attacks: Air Cutter (Flying Slash crit), Poison Fang (high poison), Confuse Ray, Bite
  > Low Atk stat → simpler. Thunderbolt (Flying) or Psychic but keep Psychic types away after switch.

#### Gengar 58 — Levitate, Ghost/Poison — 2361 Exp

- Attacks: Hypnosis, Nightmare (1/4 max HP per turn while asleep), Sludge Bomb, Shadow Ball
  > Hypnosis + Nightmare combo dangerous — Poke Flute immediately if slept; Sludge Bomb/Shadow Ball strong.

## XI4. Elite 4 Lance — Dragon Master

Walk corridor auto; reward $6000.

#### Gyarados 56 — Intimidate, Water/Flying — 2568 Exp

- Attacks: Dragon Rage (always 40), Twister (hits Fly), Bite, Hyper Beam (150 power → recharge)
  > Intimidate −Atk useless vs Thunderbolt. One Thunderbolt KO. Hyper Beam leaves free turn if you survive.

#### Aerodactyl 58 — Rock Head, Rock/Flying — 2509 Exp

- Attacks: Ancientpower (100 power, 10% raise all stats +1), Wing Attack, Scary Face, Hyper Beam
  > Fastest + high Atk. Ancientpower strong vs Fire/Flying/Ice (Lapras/Charizard). Surf/Thunderbolt both SE. Heal after Hyper Beam.

#### Dragonair 54 — Shed Skin, Dragon — 1665 Exp

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Outrage (Dragon, confuses after 2–3 turns), Thunder Wave, Safeguard (no status 5 turns)
  > Shed Skin 1/3 cure each turn → don't status. Dragon resists common types → Ice (Ice Beam) or Dragon STAB (Lapras star).

#### Dragonite 60 — Inner Focus, Dragon/Flying — 2802 Exp

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Safeguard, Outrage, Wing Attack
  > Inner Focus blocks flinch. Ice 4× weak even unSTAB kills or leaves low; massive Atk → Hyper Beam lethal if not OHKO.

#### Dragonair 54 — Shed Skin, Dragon — 1665 Exp (second)

- Attacks: Dragon Rage, Safeguard, Hyper Beam, Outrage
  > Dragon Rage 40 vs Thunder Wave swap — same counters: Ice/Dragon, don't status.

## XI5. Champion Fight! Gary Battle #8

Round room with **Gary as Champion** — title steal.

**Champion Gary Pokémon**

| Your Starter     | Team (Rhydon59 always)                                                                          |
| ---------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Bulbasaur        | Charizard63 + Pidgeot59 (2173) + Gyarados61 + Alakazam57 (2271) + Exeggutor59 + Rhydon59 (2578) |
| Charmander       | Blastoise63 + Pidgeot59 + Exeggutor59 + Alakazam57 + Arcanine61 (2784) + Rhydon59               |
| Squirtle         | Venusaur63 (2808) + Pidgeot59 + Arcanine61 + Alakazam57 + Gyarados59 (2704) + Rhydon59          |
| **Money:** $6300 |

#### Pidgeot 59 — Keen Eye, Flying/Normal — 2173 Exp

- Attacks: Aerial Ace (never misses), Sand-Attack, Featherdance (−2 Atk), Whirlwind (switch when low)
  > Sand spam → switch to reset; Featherdance avoid Physical. Thunderbolt/Ice Beam.

#### Alakazam 57 — Synchronize, Psychic — 2271 Exp

- Attacks: Psychic, Future Sight (hits 2 turns later even if dead), Recover, Reflect (halves Special)
  > Higher SpA/HP than before, Recover rarely used (idiot AI), Reflect → Psychic/Future Sight spam.

#### Gyarados 59/61 — Intimidate, Water/Flying — 2704 Exp

- Attacks: Hydro Pump (strong but missy), Dragon Rage (40), Bite, Thrash (confuses after)
  > Weak as ever vs Thunderbolt (Intimidate irrelevant).

#### Rhydon 59 — Lightningrod, Rock/Ground — 2578 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Rock Tomb, Take Down, Scary Face
  > Finally evolved from Rhyhorn — one Surf still OHKOs. Lightningrod attracts Electric (useless 1v1).

#### Arcanine 61 — Intimidate, Fire — 2784 Exp

- Attacks: Flamethrower, Extremespeed (always first), Bite, Roar (forces switch)
  > Intimidate −Atk; Extremespeed priority, Flamethrower strong, Roar switch. Surf.

#### Exeggutor 59 — Chlorophyll, Grass/Poison —

- Attacks: Giga Drain, Egg Bomb (weak inaccurate), Sleep Powder, Light Screen (+SpDef)
  > Sleep Powder → Poke Flute. Light Screen lets survive Fire/Ice without STAB.

#### Venusaur 63 — Overgrow, Grass/Poison — 2808 Exp

- Attacks: Solarbeam, Synthesis (heal, more with Sunny Day), Growth (+SpA), Sunny Day
  > Synthesis spam when Sunny Day = heavy healing; Sunny Day lets Solarbeam no-charge + boosts Fire (his weakness) → exploit Fire/Ice/Psychic.

#### Charizard 63 — Blaze, Fire/Flying —

- Attacks: Fire Blast, Aerial Ace, Fire Spin, Slash
  > Fire Blast high burn chance, Aerial Ace never misses. Rock 4× weak.

#### Blastoise 63 — Torrent, Water —

- Attacks: Hydro Pump, Surf, Rain Dance (boosts Water → Electric 100% acc), Body Slam (para chance)
  > Two Water STABs redundant; Rain Dance → Thunderbolt 100% — punish.

## XI6. The Credits and Afterwards

Defeat Gary → **Professor Oak** enters, lectures Gary (didn't respect Pokémon, never win again), congratulates you → walk to next room → **Hall of Fame** entry (team recorded) + time imprinted on Trainer Card → remixed credits.

If you **didn't** do Island quest with Bill before Victory Road, you can't progress to new Pokédex — find Bill in Center and engage island quest (covered after Elite 4).

If you **did** do Three Island, Oak inspects Dex: with **≥60 Pokémon** he takes you + Gary to lab → upgrades to **National Dex** (holds 386, including non-Kanto). This unlocks Sevii Islands as next objective.

> Want to rematch Elite 4? Too bad — **Lorelei left** Indigo Plateau after your win for unexplained reasons — find out why...

# XII. End of Game Bonuses

So you have beaten the Elite 4 and are Champion — big deal. Real challenges start now. Secrets & bonuses you may have missed or that drive players insane.

## XII1. The Legendary Beasts

After Elite 4, one roaming beast appears — music distinct (Ho-Oh/Lugia GSC rare battle theme), Lv50, huge presence. Which appears depends on starter:

| Starter Chosen | Beast Roams Kanto |
| -------------- | ----------------- |
| Bulbasaur      | Entei             |
| Charmander     | Suicune           |
| Squirtle       | Raikou            |

Like GSC Raikou/Entei or RS Lati@s — pain to catch. Even asleep they flee (sleep-walk ability).

**Capture Options:**

- **Option 1 (Recommended):** Use **Master Ball** — no hunt, no damage tracking.
- **Option 2 (Hard):** Inflict Sleep/Paralysis (DO NOT Burn/Poison — if dies, gone forever). Damage + status persist across encounters until caught/killed. On re-encounter use high-level **False Swipe** (leaves 1 HP) → pray next Ball catches.
- **NEVER** use Mean Look/Spider Web or trapping abilities vs **Raikou/Entei** — they know **Roar** (instant battle end even through traps/Move/Ability) — if they Roar, gone forever as if killed. **Suicune does NOT know Roar** → trapping fair game → easiest to catch. Note: Suicune trapped may Struggle after PP depletion → recoil suicide — be careful. Can soft-reset (off/on) if you kill by mistake.
- **Option 3:** Trade all three from **Pokémon Colosseum**.

## XII2. Powdered Prizes

No contests → Berry Crusher replaces Berry Blender. Get **Powder Jar** + prizes from man left of robbed house in Cerulean (left of Rocket-robbed house). **Berry Crusher:** Requires **Wireless Adapter** (not link cable) at **2F Center Room Center** of Pokémon Center. All players insert berry → mash **A** → faster + more players = more powder. Trade at house.

| Item         | Effect                                | Powder Cost |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------- | ----------- |
| Energypowder | Restores 50 HP (bitter)               | 50          |
| Energy Root  | Restores 200 HP (bitter)              | 80          |
| Heal Powder  | Cures all special conditions (bitter) | 50          |
| Revival Herb | Revives fainted (bitter)              | 300         |
| Protein      | +10 Atk EVs (+1 stat)                 | 1000        |
| Carbos       | +10 Speed EVs                         | 1000        |
| Iron         | +10 Defense EVs                       | 1000        |
| Zinc         | +10 Sp.Def EVs                        | 1000        |
| Calcium      | +10 Sp.Atk EVs                        | 1000        |
| HP Up        | +10 HP EVs                            | 1000        |
| PP Up        | Increases max PP of a move            | 3000        |

> Only PP Up worthwhile, but insanely expensive and not competitive-necessary.

## XII3. Celedon City Rocket Game Corner

Every game has a Game Corner for coins → prizes. Need **Coin Case** first: SE city row of buildings → leftmost café → top-left table man (tired of losses) gives Coin Case.

**Hidden Coins inside Game Corner:**

- 3 up + A → **10 Coins**
- 5 more up, face left + A → **20 Coins**
- Far left row: under 2nd row machines, 1 right + A → **10 Coins**; 6 right → another **10 Coins**
- Far left bald character: 1 north + A → **20 Coins**
- Bottom-left corner Sticker Printer: face left + A → **100 Coins**

Extra: buy **50 Coins $1000** or **500 Coins $10000** (no bulk discount), or play slots: **777 red 7s = 300 coins**, **777 blue 7s = 150**, **RRR Team Rocket = 100** (reels fast — timing or mashing works). Bottom-right Sticker Machine imprints team onto Trainer Card (cosmetic).

**Redemption Center** next door:

| Left Woman   | Price | Center Woman | Price | Right Woman                | Price |
| ------------ | ----- | ------------ | ----- | -------------------------- | ----- |
| Smoke Ball   | 800   | Abra         | 180   | TM 13 (Ice Beam)           | 4000  |
| Miracle Seed | 1000  | Clefairy     | 500   | TM 23 (Irate? Shadow Ball) | 3500  |
| Charcoal     | 1000  | Pinsir (LG)  | 5500  | TM 24 (Thunderbolt)        | 4000  |
| Mystic Water | 1000  | Scyther (FR) | 5500  | TM 30 (Iron Tail)          | 4500  |
| Yellow Flute | 1600  | Dratini      | 2800  | TM 35 (Flamethrower)       | 4000  |
|              |       | Porygon      | 9999  |                            |       |

> Left: Smoke Ball cheaper at Dept Store; Seeds/Charcoal/Mystic Water +10% type boost miniscule; Yellow Flute infinite Confuse cure. Center: rare/hard-catch Pokémon (Abra teleports, Clefairy rare, version Bugs), Dratini fishable in Safari, Porygon only buyable. Right: **must-buy TMs** — Ice Beam, Shadow Ball, Thunderbolt (every team needs), Iron Tail meh, Flamethrower excellent.

## XII6. Cerulean Cave

Renamed from Unknown Dungeon — layout same, prize **Mewtwo Lv70** end. Requirements: beat Elite 4 + complete Sevii quest up to **Sapphire Plate to Celio** (Hoenn connectivity). Bring many **Ultra Balls / Master Ball + Sleep inducer** (Sleep > Paralysis). Fly to Cerulean → over Nugget Bridge north → left surf small river south → cave guarded by dork until requirements met. West: **Swimmer Melissa** Paras×2/Parasect31 $496 → enter.

Hardest cave — wilds 45–65 deeper = higher.

**Fastest Mewtwo path:** Surf lake → follow river south → onto dry land south looping around center ladder (pass) → down **center ladder**. Path: left, south at first split, right to wall, south to split, right to wall, south, right, up → no more splits → ladder → next ladder adjacent → **bottom floor** winding turns → small river to Mewtwo platform. **Save.**

| ----------------------------- |

| Swift (never misses)          |
| Safeguard (no status 5 turns) |

> Insane SpAtk + high defenses/HP; Recover spam if you don't sleep — sleep blocks Recover → damage/Ultra Balls; Dark immune to Psychic + resists Swift → tank. Only Master Ball if you want to save it vs Beasts.

## XII7. Elite 4 Rematch

Requirements: beat first Elite 4 + have **National Dex** → talk to **Celio** on Island One → upgrades Tri-Pass → **Rainbow Pass** (all 7 Sevii). On **Island 4 Icefall Cave** find **Lorelei** cornered by Rocket stealing Pokémon → help defeat them → she explains birthplace, returns to Indigo → rematch unlocked.

Rematch: all teams **65–75**, changed movesets, much harder but huge Exp.

**Elite 4 Lorelei (Rematch)**

#### Dewgong 64 — Thick Fat — 2413 Exp

- Attacks: Surf, Ice Beam, Double Team, Signal Beam (75 power Bug, odd on Dewgong)
  > Ice Beam replaces Aurora; Double Team evasion spam → Thunderbolt.

#### Cloyster 63 — Shell Armor — 2738 Exp

- Attacks: Surf, Ice Beam, Rain Dance, Supersonic (confuse)
  > Hail→Rain Dance (now Electric always hits Cloyster — bad). Supersonic start.

#### Piloswine 63 — Oblivious, Ice/Ground — 2160 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Blizzard, Double-Edge, Rock Slide
  > Replaces Slowbro — crappy replacement per author; slow → Surf SE (Ground). Blizzard strong but low acc; Double-Edge recoil.

#### Jynx 66 — Oblivious, Ice/Psychic — 1934 Exp

- Moves unchanged: Psychic, Ice Beam, Attract, Lovely Kiss → Poke Flute vs Sleep; send female vs Attract.

#### Lapras 66 — Water Absorb, Water/Ice — 3096 Exp

- Attacks: Surf, Ice Beam, Thunder, Psychic
  > Now 300 HP, coverage destroys 11/17 types; Shell Armor → Water Absorb (Water heals Lapras). Dark walls well; don't use Water.

**Elite 4 Bruno (Rematch)**

#### Steelix 65 — Rock Head, Steel/Ground — 2710 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Iron Tail, Crunch, Rock Tomb
  > Onix evolved → loses 4× Water but gains Fire weak; mediocre stats.

#### Hitmonlee 65 — Limber — 1934 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Rock Slide, Mega Kick, Foresight
  > Earthquake for Psychic, Foresight for Mega Kick accuracy — low Speed/Def.

#### Machamp 68 — Guts — 2810 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Rock Slide, Cross Chop (high crit), Brick Break
  > Real powerhouse — high Atk; Earthquake coverage; Brick Break breaks screens.

#### Hitmonchan 65 — Keen Eye — 1950 Exp

- Attacks: Mach Punch, Sky Uppercut, Rock Slide, Counter
  > Stay away from Physical (Counter); Flying/Psychic.

#### Steelix 66 — Rock Head — 2772 Exp

- Attacks: Earthquake, Iron Tail, Dragonbreath (30% para), Crunch
  > +1 level, Dragonbreath para chance → Flamethrower/Surf.

**Elite 4 Agatha (Rematch)**

#### Gengar 66 — Levitate — 2684 Exp

- Attacks: Psychic, Shadow Ball, Hypnosis, Confuse Ray
  > Dream Eater→Confuse Ray, Double Team→Psychic. Levitate still unexplained.

#### Crobat 66 — Inner Focus, Poison/Flying — 2882 Exp

- Attacks: Sludge Bomb, Shadow Ball, Air Cutter (high crit), Confuse Ray
  > Golbat evolved → fast annoyer; no Toxic now but Confuse start → Thunderbolt/Psychic.

#### Misdreavus 65 — Levitate, Ghost — 2046 Exp

- Attacks: Psychic, Shadow Ball, Thunderbolt, Attract
  > Replaces Haunter — only pure Ghost in GSC era; Attract male more often.

#### Arbok 68 — Intimidate — 2142 Exp

- Attacks: Sludge Bomb, Earthquake, Giga Drain, Double Team
  > Double Team spam → hit fast with Psychic; Giga Drain counters Ground/Rock.

#### Gengar 70 — Levitate — 2850 Exp

- Attacks: Sludge Bomb, Shadow Ball, Psychic, Thunderbolt
  > Hypnosis/Nightmare → Psychic/Thunderbolt mixed sweeper.

**Elite 4 Lance (Rematch)**

#### Gyarados 68 — Intimidate — 3116 Exp

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Earthquake, Dragon Dance, Thunder Wave
  > Intimidate → Thunder Wave → Dragon Dance 1–2× → Hyper Beam/Earthquake; Thunderbolt still OHKO.

#### Aerodactyl 70 — Rock Head — 3030 Exp

- Attacks: Aerial Ace (never miss), Earthquake, Ancientpower, Hyper Beam
  > Coverage wide, no Surf killer.

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Dragon Claw, Earthquake, Flamethrower
  > Odd but Flamethrower threatens Ice; Ice Beam still OHKO.

#### Dragonite 72 — Inner Focus — 3362 Exp

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Ice Beam, Thunderbolt, Outrage
  > +6 levels, coverage vs Water via Thunderbolt.

- Attacks: Hyper Beam, Surf, Ice Beam, Dragon Dance
  > Replaces Dragonair — resists Fire/Water/Steel; only Dragon weak (Ice resists Water, Dragon resists Thunderbolt). Dragon Dance → Surf/Hyper Beam. Burst.

# XIII. The Sevii Islands Quest

Favorite part per author. After Cinnabar, Bill asks to go to islands off Kanto mainland. If you said **Yes** first time, only first 3 islands + part hidden areas until National Dex. If you waited until post-Elite 4 + National Dex, Bill waits in Cinnabar Center.

> Note: If you came early and just now got National Dex, continue with Rainbow Pass. If you came late with National Dex, still need to clear first 3 islands main quest before Rainbow Pass.

## XIII1. One Island — Friends Gather at Knot Island

**Quick syndicate (if skipped above):**

- First Sevii visit needs **Surf, Strength, Cut** — National Dex NOT required.
- Bill stays in Cinnabar Center until you say Yes to islands.

Say Yes → Bill rushes you on **Hydrospeed Mach X** (number varies) to **One Island**. Stuck there until you finish Three Island (requires One + Two). Bill escorts to **Pokémon Net Center** (Center + giant internet machine). **Celio** (Bill's buddy) explains Hoenn↔Kanto PC link lacks power → need **Ruby + Sapphire** slotted. Need to find them. Bill gives **Tri-Pass** (boat between 1/2/3) + **Meteorite** (R/S reference) for Two Island scientist + **Town Map** (adds Sevii pages).

You could go directly to Two, but One has Moltres etc. — read on or skip to XIII2.

**Treasure Beach** (south of boathouse, Surf):

Lower cavern has many Braille tablets: top-left ABC, next row DEF, pattern to YZ last tablet. Down stairs Braille message decodes: _"Everything has meaning. Existence has meaning. Being alive has meaning. Have dreams use power."_ → 42? Final stairs → shiny red **Ruby** → exit via east fork at earlier fork. Fly to One Center → hand Ruby to Celio → needs Sapphire → upgrades **Tri-Pass → Rainbow Pass** (all 7 islands) + updates Town Map.

Done with One — sail to Two!

## XIII4. Four Island — The Warm, Blue, Floe Island

Remixed GSC music. Out of boathouse Gary talks (not battle): brags Pokémon Egg, unknown business. West Smash rocks → **Star Piece** (sell) → town Center bookshelf → **Daisy's Fame Checker** (Daisy is Bill's sister?).

Above Center: **Day-Care** (holds 2 → Eggs!). Pair male+female same Egg Group or with Ditto → Egg is lowest evolution of female (exceptions), moves from father; with Ditto, non-Ditto parent determines egg. Legendaries/other Ditto can't breed with Ditto.

House next to Day-Care: teaches **Body Slam** (Normal, 30% paralyze).

## XIII8. Eight Island — Naval Rock

Needs Action Replay/Gameshark or Mystic Ticket event. Cave with no wilds: left path ladders up many tiny rooms → **Ho-oh Lv70** peak (Recover/Fire Blast/Swift/Safeguard), right path ladders down to pool → **Lugia Lv70** (Recover/Hydro Pump/Swift/Safeguard). No trainers, just legends; cheat to Nine.

## XIII9. Nine Island — Birth Island

Needs **Old Sea Map** (cheat). Triangle dirt puzzle at top black triangle: press **A 11 times quickly without walking into rock** — sequence moves rock bottom-left → top → bottom-right → middle-left → middle-right → bottom-middle → bottom-left → bottom-right → bottom-middle → top → spawns **Deoxys Lv30** (Snatch/Psychic/Pursuit/Knock Off). Forms differ: FireRed punch stance high Atk/SpA, LeafGreen wide arms high Def/SpDef/HP, R/S normal, Emerald Speed form high Speed.

# XIV. Sevii Islands Bonuses

Some extras post-main quest: fun vs time-wasters.

## XIV1. Seven Island's Trainer Tower

North of Seven across small river — FR/LG half-assed Battle Tower. Two Psychics block:

#### Psychic Rodette (Natu48/Drowzee48/Hypno50 $1000), Psychic Dario (Girafarig52 $1040) → across water.

4 modes vs clock, levels scale to highest party level (XX), no Exp/money, healing items allowed, one-time prize per mode:

**SINGLE** (8 floors 1 Pokémon each): Sailor Alberto Feraligatr/Kangaskhan → Bug Catcher Brandon Beedrill/Yanma → Juggler Jarrett Weezing/Exeggcute → Youngster Cole Primeape/Slowbro → Camper Joey Nidoking/Tauros → Swimmer Braden Dunsparce/Politoed → Burglar Jac Meowth\* shiny Chansey → Breeder Lily Togepi/Snorlax → prize **Upgrade** (Porygon→Porygon2)

**DOUBLE** (first 2 in party sent): Twins Jen+Kira Jolteon/Espeon\*shiny → Crush Kin Jo+Haley Alakazam/Houndoom → Ric+Rene Golem/Machamp → Cool Couple Issac+Mag Piloswine/Crobat → Jos+Anne Blissey/Arcanine → Emy+Alek Ursaring/Furret → Axe+Ren Lanturn/Dragonair → Geb+Megan Gyarados/Rhydon → prize **Dragon Scale** (Seadra→Kingdra)

**KNOCKOUT** (3 trainers ×1 each floor): Biker Jordy/Ernest/Gabriel Magby/Togepi/Smoochum; Hiker Mike/Rebecca/Nicolas Charizard/Flareon/Poliwrath; Camryn/Natalia/Kathleen Miltank/Vileplume/Lapras; Brennan/Kaden/Emanuel Chansey\* shiny/Seaking/Vaporeon; Maura/Mikaela/Flint NidoranF/Nidorina/Nidoqueen; Ben/Camden/Zackery Ampharos/Granbull/Misdreavus; Priscila/Charlotte/Shania Goldeen/Qwilfish/Mantine; Chelsea/Trenton/Albert Starmie/Arcanine/Venusaur → prize second **Metal Coat**

**MIXED** (compilation): Allyson Starmie/Kingdra → Lorenzo Hypno/Gengar → Owen Jolteon/Porygon2 → Geb+Megan Nidoking/Nidoqueen → Kat+Kipp Corsola/Kingler → Chelsea/Trenton/Albert Ledian/Gyarados/Tyranitar → Ric+Rene Hitmonlee/Hitmonchan → Mike/Rebecca/Nicolas Charizard/Nidoqueen/Poliwrath → prize **King's Rock** (flinch chance).

## XIV2. Two Island's Wireless Game Corner

On Two Island, southeast of the Pokémon Center is a house resembling Celedon Game Corner — **Wireless Game Corner**. Requires **Wireless Adapter** (GBA Cable NOT allowed). Two games with a friend wirelessly:

- **Pokémon Jump:** Any Pokémon ≤28 inches tall that can jump (no Diglett/Magikarp etc.). Two Venusaur make Vine Whip rope — time A jumps over rope; first touch loses; rope swings faster each revolution.
- **Dodrio Berry Pick:** 3 players, each controls one Dodrio head → time lunging at passing berries overhead → most points wins.

No other purpose — mediocre entertainment for a few minutes.

## XIV3. Six Island's Heracross Horn Contest

Head to **Water Path east of Six Island** northwards → small plateau house: **Heracross Fan Girl**. With **Heracross at lead** of party, she measures horn length. Beat record → **Nest Ball** (ideal for Bug types, use to catch more Heracross). Can keep beating own record, eventually impossible — then irrelevant.

## XIV4. Six Island's Altering Cave

At **northernmost point of Six Island** is **Altering Cave** — contains only **Zubat** inside. Way to change encounters: use **E-Reader with Japanese import cards** (not compatible outside Japan) — changes Pokémon found. However all are obtainable in **Emerald, Colosseum, XD**, making cave irrelevant. Mentioned for completeness.

That's it — entire game covered head to foot. If interested in competitive battling, read XV.

---

# XV. EV Training and IV Stats for Dummies

Competitive battling is highly critical but largely unknown. By utilizing proper EVs, money, and patience for excellent IVs you can create insanely powerful Pokémon. This covers surface only — see specialized guides for depth. If you don't know DV from EV, you're in right place.

## XV1. Stat Compensation

Six main stats: **HP, Speed, Defense, Special Defense, Attack, Special Attack**. When leveling, some increase 4–5 (e.g., HP), others 0–1 — why?

All Pokémon have **Base Stats** — what stats would be with neutral nature, 0 IVs, 0 EVs (leveled via Day-Care/Rare Candies only). Sum indicates power — **>600 considered Uber** (mostly legendaries: Lugia, Ho-Oh, Mewtwo etc.). Not all legendaries are Uber: Raikou/Entei/Suicune/Regis/Bird trio generally accepted. Base Stats change upon evolution (usually increase) — but not always: non-evolving Heracross has high Atk/Speed vs others; Luvdisc/Spinda abysmal despite no evo. Check base before building: wallers need high Def/SpDef, sweepers need high Atk etc.

## XV2. Attack Usage

Every Pokémon learns only **4 distinct moves** (except Ditto: 1). Don't just stack strongest — need strategy: many want a status move (Calm Mind → +1 SpA+SpDef, Barrier/Reflect etc.).

All moves have four concepts: **Base Power, Accuracy, Type, Effects**. Base Power 90 = powerful vs 60 = not. Accuracy 80–95% for strong, 100% for others; few have no accuracy (Faint Attack/Swift always hit except target Dig/Dive/Fly). **Type → STAB:** If move type matches user type → **×1.5** (dual-type still ×1.5, author says ×1.25 for dual? Standard is ×1.5 regardless — author lists ×1.25 for dual-type example: Psychic 90 ×1.25 on Latios Dragon/Psychic =112.5 → ×2 if SE =225). Special effects (Ice Beam 10% freeze etc.) calculate after damage. **PP** = uses; powerful 5–10, weak/useless 35–40; if 0, can't use.

## XV3. How EVs Are Used

EV = **Effort Value**. Every defeated Pokémon gives **1–3 EVs** corresponding to a stat (e.g., Spinda → 1 SpA). **Link battles don't give EVs.**

**How you receive:** Any Pokémon gaining experience from that defeat gets the EV points — not split. 2 attackers + 1 Exp Share → all 3 get 1 SpA EV from Spinda.

**What they do:** Invisible — track manually. Every **4 EVs = +1 stat point at level-up**. Example: 4 Spinda → next level gets +3 SpA instead of +2. Effect scales with level — need high level (76–99) to see immediate +1; but training at Lv2 still balances fully by Lv100.

**Limits:** **510 total per Pokémon**, **255 max per stat** → waste 3 (not divisible by 4) → optimal **252/252/6** (63 +63 +1). Once full, cannot gain more; stats even out by 100 regardless of when you farmed.

**Where to farm:** Check which Pokémon gives which EVs — list here: http://db.gamefaqs.com/portable/gbadvance/file/pokemon_emerald_ev_points.txt (third-gen). For Special Sweeper: Route 113 Spinda-only farming, tick marks, flee others, move to next stat at 252.

## XV4. How IVs Are Used

IV = **Individual Value**. Six stats each have IV **0–31** (31 perfect, 0 terrible). Determines how much a stat grows per level beyond Base/EV/Nature/Level. **Random** on wild encounter or Egg **handed** (not hatched). Day-Care breeding: **2 IVs from two random stats taken from one parent, 1 IV from another stat from other parent** → remaining 3 randomized → intense breeding for 31s in key stats.

**Calculation:** Need to estimate — site http://www.legendarypokemon.net/ivcalc — insert level, nature, Pokémon, current stats, EVs → approximation (more accurate at higher level; newborns show 31/0 volatility early then stabilizes).

## XV5. The Nature Dilemma

With perfect IVs + high Base + 252 EVs, one last factor: **Nature**. Each nature raises one stat **110%** and lowers another **90%** (HP never affected); 5 neutral natures do nothing.

| Nature  | 110% Stat     | 90% Stat  |
| ------- | ------------- | --------- |
| Adamant | Attack        | Sp. Atk   |
| Bashful | — (No Change) | —         |
| Bold    | Defense       | Attack    |
| Brave   | Attack        | Speed     |
| Calm    | Sp. Def       | Attack    |
| Careful | Sp. Def       | Sp. Atk   |
| Docile  | —             | —         |
| Gentle  | Sp. Def       | Defense   |
| Hardy   | —             | —         |
| Hasty   | Speed         | Defense   |
| Impish  | Defense       | Sp. Atk\* |
| Jolly   | Speed         | Sp. Atk   |
| Lax     | Defense       | Sp. Def   |
| Lonely  | Attack        | Defense   |
| Mild    | Sp. Atk       | Defense   |
| Modest  | Sp. Atk       | Attack    |
| Naive   | Speed         | Sp. Def   |
| Naughty | Attack        | Sp. Def   |
| Quiet   | Sp. Atk       | Speed     |
| Quirky  | —             | —         |
| Rash    | Sp. Atk       | Sp. Def   |
| Relaxed | Defense       | Speed     |
| Sassy   | Sp. Def       | Speed     |
| Serious | —             | —         |
| Timid   | Speed         | Attack    |

> Need 1/25 perfect nature → Day-Care **Everstone** trick: give one parent Everstone → **50% chance nature passed to egg**. Keep hatching desired nature, test IVs via level-up/Day-Care, repeat — very long patience required.

_Correction: author lists Adamant Sp.Def→Sp.Atk but standard is Sp.Atk; Impish Attack corrected to Sp.Atk._

## XV6. Items

Every competitive Pokémon holds one (no duplicates). Key choices:

- **Recovery:** **Leftovers #1** — heals **1/16 max HP before your turn** (≈6.25% per turn) → if you lose half HP, you'll be over half again forcing two more hits. **Shell Bell** — recovers **1/8 damage dealt** (e.g., 150 dmg → ~18 HP) — better only if >288 HP Leftovers outheals, so Leftovers superior for bulky; Shell Bell for low-HP heavy hitters if Leftovers taken.

- **Berries:** **Lum Berry** — auto-cures any status (used instantly when inflicted, one-time). **Chesto/Mint** — cures Sleep; great with **Rest** (full HP + clear status) — Rest+Chesto recovers instantly.

- **Stat items:** **Scope Lens** +10% crit chance (Slash even higher → 20% with lens). **Bright Powder** −10% foe accuracy (sometimes clinch). **Quick Claw** 10% to move first regardless of Speed (low but clutch).

- **Boost items:** **Choice Band** ×1.5 Attack but locks to one move until fainted. **White Herb** restores lowered stats (Screech/partner/Overheat drops) → always stays at full strength unless statused.

This is beginner basics — seek deeper guides for full competitive depth. Good luck getting perfect Pokémon.

# XVII. Fame Checker

The finale describes the lonely item in your pack that probably won't get much use: the **Fame Checker**. This gizmo highlights info on characters you've talked to throughout journey. List of all characters and where to obtain their six tidbits.

> **Note:** Once you have all six bits of info, press **Start** while highlighting that person to read a special bit of info aimed towards you from the person!

**Professor Oak**

| #   | How to Obtain                                   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | Signboard outside Oak's laboratory              |
| 2   | Talk to Oak's assistant in Oak's laboratory     |
| 3   | Talk to Oak in Oak's laboratory                 |
| 4   | Check the bookshelf in the Pokemon School House |
| 5   | Talk to Agatha in Indigo Plateau                |
| 6   | Talk to Oak's Assistant after beating Elite 4   |

**Daisy**

| #   | How to Obtain                                                        |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | Talk to Oak's assistant after beating Elite 4                        |
| 2   | Talk to woman standing next to Fan Club president                    |
| 3   | Show the guy that handed you the Egg the Togepi that hatched from it |
| 4   | Check the bookshelf in the Pokemon School House                      |
| 5   | Talk to the Old Lady in the Celedon Mansion                          |
| 6   | Check the bookshelf in the Island 4 Pokemon Center                   |

**Brock**

| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | Gym Sign next to Pewter City Gym                                                                                                                     |
| 2   | Talk to Brock                                                                                                                                        |
| 3   | Talk to the guy in front of Pewter Pokemon Center                                                                                                    |
| 4   | Talk to boy in front of Mt. Moon Pokemon Center                                                                                                      |
| 5   | Talk to man inside Mt. Moon first floor                                                                                                              |
| 6   | Inspect bookshelf inside Pewter Museum in the area where you got the Old Amber, only available if you have the other five Brock Fame Checker entries |

---

# XVIII. Closing Words

Well, that's it for another one of my guides. There isn't much more to say or accomplish that's left in the game. Continue raising your Pokemon for competitive internet gameplay when Diamond/Pearl swing around, attempt grabbing those dozen stickers on Four Island, do whatever now. I'm glad you chose this guide to get the job done.
